**The layout scripts** (`tools/UiFit/hand/<Window>.py`, rows in `grid-hand/6.tsv` with rule `by hand (layout script): ...`; each script's
header says what the planner left and what the script does). `hand/_edges.py` holds their common steps: `frame()` moves a content's left,
right (and bottom) edges to the padding (a wide control keeps its other edge), `bottom_to`, `over` and `right_to` put an edge against
another's with the grid's gaps. What the scripts fix, by kind:

| Kind | Windows |
|---|---|
| Margins: main's 11 to 27 px left margin and right edges 4 to 20 px short (the planner keeps anchored grids and the controls on their edges where main drew them), bottom rows 20 to 40 px over the bottom | FormClinics, FormCodeGroups, FormCodeSystemsImport, FormEcwDiagAdv, FormEhrEduResourcesPat, FormEhrLabNoteEdit, FormEmailAutographEdit, FormEtrans270EBraw, FormInnoDb, FormInsFilingCodeEdit, FormMedLabPatSelect, FormPatFieldDefs, FormPayorTypes, FormPayPlanRecalculate, FormProcCodesOther, FormProgramLinks, FormProvStudentBulkEdit, FormQueryEdit, FormQueryParser, FormRedundantIndexes, FormReminderRules, FormRpUdsPlusSetup, FormRxNorms, FormSearchPatNotes, FormSheetProcSelect, FormShutdown, FormSupplyReceive, FormTreatPlanAddProcs, FormWikiAllPages, FormWikiSearch, FormGlidewellSetup, FormImageCatMerge, FormEmailAddresses, FormElectIDEdit, FormCanadaSummaryReconciliation, FormMarkupTableEdit |
| Side-by-side grids or lists: one width, one top and one bottom, 8 px apart, the arrow buttons centred between them | FormDashboardWidgetSetup, FormFeeSchedGroupEdit, FormFeeSchedGroups, FormFieldDefLink, FormHL7Defs, FormSheetDefs, FormSupplyProcCodes, FormTreatPlanCurEdit, FormRequiredFields (three columns on one top), FormInsPlanSelectFam |
| Empty bands: grids down to 8 px over their button row, bottom rows on the padding | FormClaimOverpay, FormLetterEdit, FormMaintHistory, FormSupplyCount, FormSupplyInventory, FormTaskInboxSetup, FormTerminalManager |
| Cut or covered text: notes as tall as their lines, labels as wide as their texts (also the run-time ones) | FormAutomationEdit (the two-line Procedure Code(s) label, Action Object's run-time captions), FormEhrClinicalSummary and FormEhrElectronicCopy ("includes 2 files" cut at "viewin"), FormEmailTemplateEdit (the three-line replacement fields caption), FormUnschedListSetup, FormTaskInboxSetup (the grid was drawn over the instructions, main too), SheetEditMobileCtrl, FormDatabaseMaintInvalidChars, FormPrinterEdit (the rule line), UserControlSetupWizOperatory (the red-items note over the instructions, main too) |
| Rows and columns the planner placed badly | FormAutomationEdit (Delete, a local of InitializeComponent the planner does not read, beside Save), FormClaimPayTotal (the totals row of abutting boxes the code keeps, the captions over their buttons), FormDatabaseMaintTemp (the step list), FormEhrLabResultEdit (the groups at the padding, one field column), FormEhrPatientSelectSimple (the search rows at the top), FormEhrTimeSynch, FormImagePickerDXC (the right column), FormInventorySetup (the check box was put on the field column), FormInvoiceItemSelect (All and None on their bottom anchor), FormJournal (the filter pairs 16 px apart), FormPopupDisplay, FormQueryMonitor (the button column from the top, Copy on its bottom anchor), FormReactivationSetup, FormSheetOutputFormat, FormSpellCheck (Delete on its bottom anchor), FormSupplies (Up over Down, the order group, Print and its note), FormTimeCardRuleEdit (one field column, the check boxes a list), FormXChargeReconcile and FormRpPHRawPop (the calendars at their drawn 190 x 176) |
| Window sizes the planner broke, back to main's | FormMaintRecordEdit (grown from 760 to 1033 px for the Date done row of run-time lines: one row of the due line, Date done and the done-by line in main's width), FormSupplyStock (grown from 1084 to 1636 px with an empty middle), FormQueryParser (grown to 839 x 718: past the 720 px window height its Show Text button sets, which would have hidden OK), FormQueryMonitor (grown by 22 x 14), FormPopupDisplay (grown from 425 to 634 px for a group that is never shown) |

**The tool.** No planner rule was added. `grid_tools.py apply` finds the `Location` and `Size` line of a control the designer declares as a
local of InitializeComponent (`butDelete.Location`, no `this.`: FormAutomationEdit's Delete), so a hand row can move it. **Found here, left
to the lead:** the planner places a `SplitContainer`'s two `SplitterPanel`s as containers (FormQueryParser and FormQueryMonitor: moved to 8, 8
with the 8 px padding and grown, and the window grown for that), though the container places its panels itself; the scripts put them back
(0, 0 and under the splitter) and the windows at main's size. The planner also grows a window to hold a control hidden in the designer that
no code shows (FormPopupDisplay's Disable for group).

**What the code positions or sizes** (checked in each `.cs`): FormClaimPayTotal's Load moves the totals boxes left by one box outside Canada
(`LayoutManager.MoveLocation`, each at the one before's `Right-1`): the script keeps the designer row so (63 px boxes, 62 px apart);
FormWikiCompare's Load sizes and places the two text boxes and their labels by the divider (the planner refuses the window, it stays as on
main); FormQueryParser's Show Text sets the window's height to 325 or 720 (the designer's 681 client height is main's, so 720 shows OK);
FormJournal, FormSupplies and FormWikiSearch set their height (FormWikiSearch also its width) from the screen; SheetEditMobileCtrl adds and
sizes its preview controls in code (its top row only is on the grid). No other window of the slice positions controls in code.
**Run-time texts**: 22 rows in `grid-runtime-texts/6.tsv` (Action Object's captions, the DXC character counts, the invalid characters
explanation, its summary and its cannot-fix line, Glidewell's recorded-by line, the maintenance item, due and done-by lines, the printer
rule and status, the lab e-mail check box, the stock count, supply and count lines, the kiosk's patient and forms captions, the time card
rule's Employee caption, the treatment plan line, the wiki search limit), widths measured by GridRulesTests; none is cut.
`FitGallery.FillSampleContent` shows the first run-time text of the placeholder labels of seven windows (FormDatabaseMaintInvalidChars,
FormMaintHistory, FormMaintRecordEdit, FormTreatPlanAddProcs, FormSupplyStock, FormPrinterEdit, FormTerminalManager), so their pictures show
what the rows were sized for.

**Window sizes** (client area, designer px; every one within 1920 x 1080): 51 designers grew to their content, most by 1 to 40 px (the larger: FormPayPlanRecalculate 379 x 186 -> 379 x 234, FormPrinterEdit 516 -> 576 wide for the rule line, FormQueryEdit 670 -> 727 tall, FormSupplies 657 -> 724 tall (its code sets the height from the screen), FormEhrPatientSelectSimple 530 -> 578 tall, DashIndividualDiscount 170 x 84 -> 174 x 108 (fix round: its hosts take that size)); FormMaintRecordEdit, FormSupplyStock, FormQueryParser, FormQueryMonitor and FormPopupDisplay keep main's size; in the fix round two shrank against `main` by the reviewer's findings (FormEhrLabResultEdit 390 x 338 -> 390 x 303, FormBlockoutCutCopyPaste 290 x 383 -> 219 x 360: the empty bands main drew; neither window's code sets its size).

**Rows.** `grid-hand/6.tsv`: 725 rows of the 83 scripts. `grid-known/6.tsv`: 1 row (FormPopupDisplay's Disable for group, hidden in the designer and shown by no code, keeps main's place past the window's right edge). `grid-known/pilot.tsv` and `grid-known/1.tsv` (the
pilot's and slice 1's files; GridRulesTests fails on a known row that no longer happens): ControlAccount's and ControlTreat's overlap of
DashIndividualDiscount's two boxes, gone now that its designer is on the grid. `fit-fixes-superseded/6.tsv`: 150 rows (of the 154 rows of the slice's windows) rows.
`layout-snapshot/6.tsv` written from the final build. The UiFit Python tests: 17 of 22 pass; the same five as in slice 4 (the batch plan's
test and four SplitTests) wait for the lead's `plan_batches.py` and `split_grid_data.py` reruns after the merge.

**Planner and the earlier windows.** From the final build's tree (`<W>/tree-all.tsv`, every window of `grid-windows`): the pilot's 47
windows plan 243 rows, slice 1's 461, slice 2's 179 and slice 3's 37, the same rows as slice 3's fix round re-runs
(`G-UI3-ui12-3-src/work/plan-rerun-*-a8.tsv`); slice 4's 104 windows plan the same rows as its fix round's `replan-f2.tsv` (240). This slice's

plan and win); **the 14 windows without a script plan 0 rows**. `grid_tools.py anchors` lists no bottom-anchored control in the upper half.

OPMS_UI_GRID_DUMP=<W>/tree-all.tsv OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY=<W>/final-100 OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY_FONT=program OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY_WINDOWS=<slice 6> OPMS_UI_LAYOUT_SNAPSHOT_WRITE=1 (Targeted UiRefreshTests)

`<W>/refit.sh` re-laid each window out from the branch point (designer back to `main`, its planner rows, its script on `<W>/tree-planned.tsv`,
its rows of `grid-hand/6.tsv`).

ControlAccount's popup groupBoxIndDis 174 x 108 at 575, 64 (its right edge on the insurance summaries' 749; at 577 it would touch the Show tab control at 750) by a hand row in `grid-hand/pilot.tsv`. ### Slice 7 (batch 3)

Branch `ui/ui12-7-1` from `main` (`b5f360847`). Batch 3 of [UI-12-batches.tsv](UI-12-batches.tsv) at the branch point (the small windows,
120 designer files), run with the UI-12T tooling (section 2.1). Slice 6 ran at the same time in another worktree (none of its windows is
in batch 3), so this slice wrote only its own files (`Data/<folder>/7.tsv`, its scripts in `tools/UiFit/hand/`, its windows' designers),
the shared docs' own sections, `grid-hand/3.tsv` (the slice 3 re-plan, below) and `UI-12-batches.tsv` (its 120 rows out).
`<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-7-src/work`.

**The windows** (`grid-windows/7.tsv`, slice 7, mode tool: 120 windows, none skipped). The planner and its templates lay out every one
from the branch point's tree (the anchors LayoutManager stores, slice 5); 104 get a layout script on the planner's tree
(`<W>/tree-planned.tsv`, the first after build; 96 in the first round, 8 more in the fix round below), 16 keep the planner's layout

### Slice 7, fix round (the lead's review of 2026-11-23, 28 findings)

Branch `ui/ui12-7-2` from `ui/ui12-7-1` (`f6bc8f240`), same worktree. Every finding is real; each window is laid out again from the
branch point by `work/refit.sh <window>` (its designer back to `b5f360847`, its planner rows, its layout script on `work/tree-planned.tsv`,
its rows of `grid-hand/7.tsv`). Eight windows that kept the planner's layout get a script (FormAllergyEdit, FormDiscountPlans,
FormGradingScales, FormImageSelectClaimAttach, FormOrthoChartSetup, FormDashboardWidgets, FormImageSelect, FormMedications), 19 scripts
change; each script's header ends with the fix round's sentence. No planner rule and no `_s7.py` helper changed.

One rule for the side columns, from the findings: the buttons of a side column stand on the bottom bar's button (OK or Save) at its x
and width, the column on the content's right edge; a check box in the column (wider than the buttons) ends on that edge; the grid (and a
search box or clinic picker over it) ends 8 px before the column's leftmost control and starts on the content edge (8; several grids
stood at 12 or 13).

|---|---|---|

Branch `ui/ui12-8-2` from `ui/ui12-8-1`. Every finding was real; each is fixed in a layout script (eight new scripts, eight of the
slice's changed), in FormInsHistSetup's code, or in the gallery, and `<W>/refit.sh` re-laid each window out from the branch point.

| # | Window | Finding | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | FormInsHistSetup | The code's rows on literal numbers off the grid (24 px pitch for 23 px boxes, labels 1 px from the boxes, 22 px buttons at y - 1, Save 14 px from the right, a 12 px bottom margin); the gallery showed no row | `AddCategoryRows` with `CodeBase.UiGrid` (above, "What the code positions or sizes"); new script `FormInsHistSetup.py` (heading 8..522, captions at the slots' x 140 and 334, Save on the right padding); new gallery state `FormInsHistSetup#rows`: `FitGallery.FillSampleCache` puts six code groups in the cache and `WindowBuilder.ActionBeforeLayoutManager` runs `AddCategoryRows` between InitializeComponent and the layout manager, as the constructor does (drawn at 100% and 150%) |
| 2 | FormLanguagesUsed | The Custom Language group a stock GroupBox (the last in OpenPmsDental), 37 px right margin, its edges on nothing | `OpenPmsDental.UI.GroupBox` (designer type; `UI-13-inventory.tsv` regenerated: the stock GroupBox row gone); the group under the All Languages list at its width, both 288 (8..296: at 262 GridRulesTests' measure wraps two of the note's three lines), the text box and Add on its content edges, its bottom on Save's |
| 3 | FormHouseCalls | The group ended at 401, its buttons 37 px in, an 87 px band over OK | The group 8..478, its buttons on its content edge; `s.spread` (two equal gaps) |
| 4 | FormDrCeph | Rows packed to the top, a 72 px band, Browse 38 px short, captions centred | New script: the lists take the spare height, captions BottomLeft at the lists' x, the rows at the pitch with the section gap over Save, path boxes and Browse on the right padding |
| 5, 6 | FormOrthoRxEdit, FormEhrAptObsEdit | 107 and 82 px bands under the rows | `s.spread` over the rows and the bar (equal 38 and 23 px gaps) |
| 7 | FormRpPHRawScreen | Calendars in the corner, 93 px band under, 136 px empty right | The calendar row centred in the content area both ways (main's arrangement) |
| 8 | FormRpReferrals | All and None 18 px short of the list; 9 / 25 px page margins | Each 89 wide, None ending on the list's edge, on the page's bottom padding; the list down to 4 px over them |
| 9 | FormEhrPatListElementEdit | Lists cutting a row (Equals, Lab result) | Whole rows of UI.ListBox's 17 px line: Restriction 4 rows (72), Operand all 3 (55, no scroll bar); the rows under them moved down |
| 10 to 15 | FormGlidewellAcctEdit, FormMessageReplacementTextEdit, FormInsSpanEdit, FormMountainside, FormInsPayFix, FormPatFieldCheckEdit | Edges 4 to 32 px off the padding or the bar; a check box's name 11 px from its box | New scripts: the list, the editor, the two code boxes (111 each), the three boxes on the group's content edge, the note and Run on the padding; the name at the check box's text lead (x 22) |

Branch `ui/ui12-11-1` from `main` (`42cc8ea3f`, main with slice 8). The lead's list (`slice11-files.tsv`, 72 designer files: the large
batches' windows and the windows earlier slices skipped), not one batch; slices 9, 10 and 12 ran at the same time in other worktrees and
none of their windows is here. This slice wrote only its own files (`Data/<folder>/11.tsv`, its scripts in `tools/UiFit/hand/`, its
windows' designers and two of their `.cs` files), `fit_tools.py`, one method of `FitGallery.cs`, `UI-12-batches.tsv` (its 26 laid-out
files' rows out), `UI-13-inventory.tsv` (regenerated: FormEhrLabImageEdit's splitter) and its own doc sections.

**The lead's decisions of slice 8.**
- `fit_tools.window_files` falls back to the designer file that declares the class when no designer file has the class's name (outside
  `--code-files` too): FormConfirmationSetup (FormConfirmationsSetup.designer.cs), FormCvxs (FormCvx.designer.cs), FormDoseSpotPropertyEdit
  (FormDoseSpotProperyEdit.designer.cs) and FormProcCodes (FormProcCodes2.designer.cs) are planned, scripted and drawn. No earlier window
  changes (each had a designer file of its name). The three get a layout script, so `grid-hand/11.tsv` names their files and the layout
  snapshot puts their rows in `layout-snapshot/11.tsv` (the snapshot's owner rule reads the file name: without a hand row the file stays
  `unsliced`).
- FormEhrLabImageEdit: its stock `System.Windows.Forms.SplitContainer` is `OpenPmsDental.UI.SplitContainer` with two `UI.SplitterPanel`s
  (the grid in Panel1, the preview in Panel2), the same orientation, splitter distance (193) and handlers (`SplitterMoved` is an
  `EventHandler` on the program's container: `splitContainer_SplitterMoved(object,EventArgs)`); the program's container keeps Panel1's size
  when it resizes, as the stock `FixedPanel.Panel1` did. The harness builds it now; laid out by a script at main's 784 x 589.
- The section gap over the bottom bar is 12 px in every script of this slice (`hand/_s11.py`, `SECTION`).

**What the code positions or sizes** (checked in each `.cs`). FormQueryFavorites sets its width (500 or 1100 px) and the splitter;

width for the paper and places the page panel and the scroll bar; FormProcCodeNoteEdit places the time bar's slider
(`TableTimeBar.GetSliderLocation`, relative to the bar, which moved with it); FormWikiListItemEdit places its in-cell combo box. Code that
placed controls with literal numbers off the grid now uses `CodeBase/UI/UiGrid.cs` (same behaviour, grid numbers): **FormWikiDrafts**
(`ResizeControls`: the working area was `294, 12, Width-397, Height-24`; now from the line numbers' box, whose right 18 px the text box
covers as on main, to `UiGrid.SIBLING_GAP` before the button column, `UiGrid.PAD` from the top and the bottom), **FormWikiHistory**
(`ResizeControls` put the text box at the grid's width + 6, as if the grid stood at 0, so the box covered the grid's right 6 px on main;
now `UiGrid.ITEM_GAP` after the grid's right edge, both boxes to `UiGrid.PAD` before the right edge) and **FormPrintTrojan** (the scroll
bar at the page panel's top instead of 33, 2 px over the panel). Texts the code sets: 8 rows in `grid-runtime-texts/10.tsv`
(FormWikiListEdit's "Advanced Search", FormRxAlertEdit's three alert captions, FormProcTools' two Canadian tools, FormPerioGraphicalSetup's
Recession, FormRxSelect's selection-mode note); the long formatted notes (FormRepeatChargesUpdate's description, FormPreAuthReminder's
carriers, FormXConnectValidation's header, FormTaskListSelect's pick note) wrap in boxes sized for them.

### Slice 10, fix round (the lead's review of 2026-11-23, 14 findings)

Branch `ui/ui12-10-2` from `ui/ui12-10-1` (`8bec9da8d`), same worktree. Each window is laid out again from the branch point by
`work/refit.sh <window>` (its designer back to `9817dee17`, its planner rows, its layout script on `work/tree-planned.tsv`, its rows of
`grid-hand/10.tsv`). Two windows that kept the planner's layout get a script (FormSupplyOrderEdit, FormSigElementDefEdit: 83 scripts, 3
windows keep the planner's layout), 7 scripts change; each script's header ends with the fix round's sentence. No planner rule and no
`_s10.py` helper changed. Twelve findings are real and fixed; two are not (the evidence below). Five windows now end 12 px under their
last control and FormTrackNextSetup ends 8 px after its group, so six windows are smaller than on `main` (the review's measure: the
empty band was the planner's compacted rows inside main's size; none of their `.cs` sets the size); `UI-12-audit-10.md`'s "a window never shrinks below main" gives way to it here.

| # | Window | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | FormPrintTrojan | Real: the page counter (AutoSize on main; the code sets `"Page: "+(CurPage()+1)+"\\"+totalPages`, `CalculatePageOffset`) was 44 px. A `grid-runtime-texts/10.tsv` row ("Page: 12\12", 68 px; the digits are one width, so "Page: 99\99" measures the same) sizes it by the script's `h.lw` to 72 px and lets GridRulesTests set the text and check it; 6 px after the buttons (161), centred on their row. |
| 2 | FormWikiListEdit | Real. The groups' button column 72 px (the groups 88 wide, on the right padding at 856; the grid 840 wide to 8 px before them); L and R 33 px each, 6 px apart, span it (8..79). The review's 32 + 32 at 865 and 903 end 1 px short of a 71 px column (71 - 6 is odd), so the column grew by 1. |
| 3 | FormSupplyOrderEdit | Real: Date Placed 100 px like Date Received (both short dates, `ToShortDateString`); its note 6 px after it (272). The note (445 px on one line) then needs 8 px more: the window 715 -> 723 wide (Save at 640). |
| 4 | FormPayPlanChargeEdit | Not real: Date Entry and Date Edit are read-only date-and-time boxes, not date boxes like Date. `FormPayPlanChargeEdit.cs` fills them with `POut.DateT(...,false)`, which is `SOut.DateT`'s `"yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"` (19 characters, about 120 px with the box's margins); at 100 px the time is cut. They keep `main`'s 128 px (the designer's on `main` too); Date, Principal and Interest keep 100. |
| 5, 6 | FormSigElementDefEdit | Real: Record New as wide as Windows Recorder (108); the Type list as wide as the Text box (104), so the note after the box keeps its place. The audit of the fix also found a 47 px empty band over Delete and Save: the planner kept the empty `label7` (no text in the designer or the code, as on `main`) 73 px tall over the bar. The label now spans the Light rows (238, 50 px tall), Delete and Save 12 px under Light Color (300), the window 599 x 334 (was 369; 363 on `main`). |
| 7, 8 | FormRxAlertEdit | Real: the default-alert note at 62 (its right edge 316, the label column's); Delete and Save 12 px under the check box (250), the window 697 x 282 (was 346 high). |
| 9 | FormRepeatChargeEditMulti | Real: Run 12 px under the last note (234) and on the right padding (562), the window 645 x 266 (was 305). |
| 10 | FormTranslationEdit | Real: Save 12 px under Comments (552), the window 856 x 586 (was 621; the boxes keep 130 px). |
| 11 | FormReplicationEdit | Real: Delete and Save 12 px under the Temp Tables note (its box ends at 284: 296; the review's 291 measured the text, not the box), the window 796 x 328 (was 353). |
| 12 | FormPerioGraphicalSetup | Not real as proposed: "Clinical Attachment Level" measures 142 px at the program's font with the Label's flags (`text_width` of the tree dump, the measure GridRulesTests and every layout script use; the review's 125 measured the ink in the picture). At 133 px the label wraps in its 23 px row and the text is cut. The column keeps the slice's label rule (the widest text + 4, snapped: 148); the 15 px between the label's left edge and the ink is the label's own padding and right alignment, as in every label column. |
| 13 | FormTrackNextSetup | Real: the Examples notes as wide as their text (136), the group from the padding to 8 px after them (8..300), the window 308 wide (was 376), Save on the right padding (225). |
| 14 | FormPrintTrojan | Real: the three icon buttons one width (45) at 8, 59 and 110. |

**Rows.** `grid-hand/10.tsv`: 545 script rows of the 83 scripts; `grid-runtime-texts/10.tsv`: 9 rows (FormPrintTrojan's page

715 -> 723 wide; FormTrackNextSetup 376 -> 308 wide; FormRxAlertEdit 346 -> 282, FormRepeatChargeEditMulti 305 -> 266,
FormTranslationEdit 621 -> 586, FormReplicationEdit 353 -> 328, FormSigElementDefEdit 369 -> 334 high.

FormTreatmentPlanDiscount (no script) keep the planner's rows as hand rows (rule `by hand (slice 9): the planner's row as a hand row ...`).
`grid_tools.apply_row` takes an `Anchor` hand row (below).

**What the code positions or sizes** (checked in each `.cs`): FormMsgBox moves OK onto Cancel's place when it hides Cancel (kept: the two
on one bar); FormReqFieldCondEdit puts the two value boxes on the operator boxes' rows and takes 70 px off the window (the operator column
on main's order keeps both under the lists); FormStateAbbrEdit takes 30 px off when it hides the Medicaid row (the last row); FormWebBrowserPrefs,
FormComboPicker, ToolTipOD and UserControlDashboardWidget size themselves (no control placed). Texts the code sets: 9 rows in
`grid-runtime-texts/9.tsv` (FormPdmpClientCertificate's clinic line, FormRecurringChargeFailEmailTemplate's replacement tags, FormSheetFieldSpecial's
two notes, FormUserPassword's two row names, FormUspsAddressSetup's three secret statuses); GridRulesTests sets each and none is cut.
`grid-known/9.tsv`: FormProcButtons' Quick Buttons note over the buttons list (the code shows one or the other) and
FormReport_GuarantorAllocationCheck's stock TextBox (unframed, as on main).

**Window sizes** (client area; every one within 1920 x 1080): FormQBAccountSelect 13 px taller (its own bar under the second box),
FormTimePick 26 px taller (the AM/PM list), FormPreferences and several dialogs taller by the bottom bar's section gap; FormMsgBox at main's
284 x 104 (the planner's 158 back). None is smaller than on main.

  text's width at most the width less the icon and its 6 px inset); a narrower one keeps the icon alone. `ButtonIconTextTests` (3 tests):

### Slice 12 (the plain exes and the graph, the final slice)

Branch `ui/ui12-12-1` from `main` (`42cc8ea3f`). The lead's list `slice12-files.tsv` (42 designer files): the plain exes' windows (Central
Manager, the MySQL user manager, the database integrity check, the documentation builder, the CRUD generator, the HL7 debug window) and the
OpenPmsDentalGraph files (the dashboard dialogs, the graph controls and their option controls), which slices 1 to 8 left for this slice.
Slices 9, 10 and 11 ran at the same time in other worktrees; none of their windows is here. This slice wrote only its own files
(`Data/<folder>/12.tsv`, its scripts in `tools/UiFit/hand/`, its windows' designers) and, for the plain windows, the tools and tests below.
`<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-12-src/work`.

**Plain windows in the tools and tests** (section 1, "Plain windows"). The harness already built them (`WindowBuilder.ArrayPlainExes` and
`ArrayPlainLibraries`: a plain window is built as a bare `Form`, a plain library's UserControl in a plain test form). What changed:
- `GridRulesTests.IsPlain(ScanType)`: a window the harness builds as `PlainWindow` or `UserControlPlain`. `CheckWindow` skips the input
  frame rule (`unframed`) for it: FormODBase attaches `UiInputFrame`, a plain form has none. Every other rule holds with the same numbers.
  New test `PlainWindow_EveryRuleButTheInputFrame` (a plain form's framed text box is no finding, the same box in a program window is one;
  an overlap is a finding in both). The text check's detail line now names the control's preferred width (for a stock check box).
- `grid_tools.py`: `is_plain_window` (the designer in a plain exe's or library's folder, no code file deriving the class from FormODBase:
  FormCentralSecurity and slice 4's FormCentralReportSetup are FormODBase windows and plan as before); `plain_client_root` (a plain form's
  tree root is its bounds, frame and title bar included: the root takes its client size, so the bottom bar stands inside the client area;
  the first plan put OK and Cancel under it); the stock check box and radio button lead 22 in a plain window (`check_width`: Windows draws
  them, their preferred width is the text + 22 to 25; GridRulesTests wraps the text in the width less what the preferred width adds);
  `window_files` finds a window whose designer code is in its own `.cs` file (FormCentralChooseDatabase, FormCentralLogOn) or in a designer
  file named otherwise than the class (MySQLManager's FormMySQLUserManager in FormMySQLManager.Designer.cs), and `runtime_positioned` no
  longer reads InitializeComponent's own lines as run-time positioning; the applier edits a `SplitterDistance` line (a hand row:
  `Hand.splitter`, the graph's settings panels).
- `grid_hand.py`: the same plain-window state; `hand/<namespace>.<class>.py` first (two exes have a `Form1`); `Hand.splitter`.
- Fix round (`ui/ui12-12-2`): `grid_tools.box_left_text_align` (a plain window's stock check box or radio button turned box-left gets a
  TextAlign row, MiddleRight -> MiddleLeft, from the planner and from `Hand.check_left`; Python test `BoxLeftTextAlignTests`);
  `BuiltWindow.LayOutAt` scales a `PlainWindow`'s or `UserControlPlain`'s bounds by the change of scale (`Control.Scale`, the form's
  client size at its whole scaled size), so the plain windows join the 150% galleries (test `PlainWindow_LayOutAt150_ScalesBounds`).
- `Run-Slice.ps1 -BuildProjects`: the before and after steps build the plain exes' projects too (the harness reads their assemblies from
  their own bins). `FitGallery` names two windows of one class name `<namespace>-<class>.png`.
- The 44 `FlatStyle.System` controls of the Central Manager and MySQL Manager windows (group boxes, labels, check boxes, buttons) take the
  default Standard style (one deleted designer line each): Windows' classic drawing has no theme, a System group box drew no frame or title
  in the harness (FormCentralProdInc's Date Range and Report Type, the search columns' Search by), and a System check box asks for another
  width than it draws.

**The windows** (`grid-windows/12.tsv`, slice 12, mode tool: 42). The planner lays out every one; 37 then get a layout script on the
planner's tree (`<W>/tree-planned.tsv`; 38 after the fix round, GraphIncome's own), with the common steps in `hand/_s12.py` (label + field rows from the padding, a label 6 px before
its field, captions, notes as tall as their lines, side-by-side groups on one top and height, on slice 8's `_s8.py` bar, spread and size);

opened: [UI-12-audit-12.md](UI-12-audit-12.md). The 42 files' rows are taken out of `UI-12-batches.tsv`. None skipped: the harness builds
every window of the list.

### Slice 13 (the windows the tools could not reach)

Branch `ui/ui12-13-1` from `main` (`28ca3702b`, main with slice 11). The lead's list (the owner: "Yes add it"): the seven windows the
harness could not build, the report designer's dialogs and controls and ODR's windows whose designer code is in their `.cs` files, and
slice 11's snapshot ownership question. Slices 9, 10 and 12 ran at the same time; none of their windows is here (slices 9 and 10 have the
report designer's other files). `<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-13-src/work`.

**1. Harness sample data** (`tests/UiRefreshTests/Scan/SampleData.cs`, harness only, no database). `WindowBuilder.Build` runs
`SampleData.BeforeLayoutManager` after InitializeComponent and before InitializeLayoutManager: FormEtrans835ClaimEdit and
FormEtrans835ProcEdit get an ERA claim or procedure with nothing reported (their Resize handlers refill four and three grids from it);
FormMountDefEdit a four-image 1200 x 600 mount (SizeChanged's CalcRatio and the drawing's Paint; `_selectedIndex` as declared);
FormSheetDefEdit an empty letter sheet of one page and the margins of the field's declaration (Resize's SetPanelMainSize, the sheet's
Paint); FormSchedule an empty week table and the work week (Resize's FillGrid(false) reads the table Load fills, else the database);
FormEhrCarePlans' Resize handler (it refills the grid from the database, EhrCarePlans.Refresh) is replaced by the column part of
FillCarePlans. FormRecallList and FormSchedule: the clinic pickers' own no-database mode (`IsTestModeNoDb`, every picker field, the ones
on tab pages not shown too); FormRecallList's pickers read EasyNoClinics in their IncludeAll setters during InitializeComponent, so
`SampleData.PrefsForInitializeComponent` puts that one preference in the cache while InitializeComponent runs and leaves the cache as it
was. PictureMapTests: FormSheetDefEdit builds now; its ButtonNewTP button is a local of DrawFields, not a control (`_arrayLocalsInCode`).

**2. Code-file designers.** `fit_tools.window_files` finds the `.cs` file with InitializeComponent for a window no designer file declares,
without `--code-files` (UI-2b's ranking keeps its flag). `fit_tools.initialize_component_span` finds InitializeComponent's body (braces
counted outside strings, verbatim strings, characters and comments); `grid_tools.Designer` reads only that body of a code file and the
applier edits only that body (every byte around it, the licence headers and the code, stays: checked for the 15 files against the branch
point), and the run-time position and window-size scans leave it out (`fit_tools.runtime_code`). Tests: `CodeFileTests` (6, among them a
byte-for-byte apply whose second run changes nothing, and the plain-Form rule below).

**The plain-Form rule** (slice 12 adds the same for the plain exes; the lead reconciles): a plain Form's tree root is the window with its
frame (GridDump's bounds) while its controls stand in the client area; `grid_tools.load_tree` lays out a root whose frame is 8 px or more on
both axes in its client area, and `write_tree` writes a planned root's client area as its planned size (so a planned tree is not read as
framed). GridRulesTests' input-frame rule (`unframed`) holds for FormODBase windows only (the frame is FormODBase's): the report
designer's stock text boxes keep their look.

**3. Snapshot ownership.** `SliceData.SnapshotOwners` and `slice_data.snapshot_owners` also give a designer file to the slice of a window
whose class the file declares (FormCvxs in FormCvx.designer.cs), not only by its file name or a hand row (`DeclaredClasses`, read from the
repository; tests on both sides: `SnapshotOwners_TheClassTheFileDeclares`, `test_snapshot_owner_by_the_declared_class`,
`test_declared_classes_reads_the_file`). `split_grid_data.py --check`: no file changes.

**The windows** (`grid-windows/13.tsv`, slice 13, mode tool: 21 windows, each with a layout script on the planner's planned tree
`<W>/tree-planout.tsv`; `hand/_s13.py` for their common steps on slice 11's `_s11.py`; each script's header says what the planner left and
what it does): FormEhrCarePlans, FormEtrans835ClaimEdit, FormEtrans835ProcEdit, FormMountDefEdit, FormRecallList, FormSchedule,
FormSheetDefEdit, ODR's FormParameterInput, and the report designer's ChartAxisCtl, DialogDatabase, DialogNewChart, DialogNewTable,

DialogDatabase, DialogNewChart, DialogToolOptions, FindTab, InteractivityCtl, PositionCtl, ReportCtl, StyleBorderCtl, StyleCtl,

**What the code positions or sizes** (checked in each `.cs`): FormEtrans835ClaimEdit and ProcEdit widen Date Service to 160 for a date
range (unchanged; the column has the room). FormSheetDefEdit sizes the window from the sheet (Width = sheet + 315, Height = sheet + 65,
within the screen) and places the sheet panel (SetPanelMainSize): the tool column keeps main's 284 px and the sheet panel is untouched.
FormMountDefEdit fits the mount left of panelRight (CalcRatio): the drawing and panelRight keep main's bounds. FormParameterInput sets its
height from the parameters it adds to panelMain (Height - panelMain.Height kept as the frame and the bar). FormRecallList hides Site when
there are no sites (unchanged).

(as wide as its text or the box); FormEtrans835ClaimEdit keeps its one space-padded caption string and puts each box's left edge 3 px
before its caption word and each sign's glyph under the caption's sign (where TextRenderer draws them at Segoe UI 9, measured in the

group labels start at the group's content edge (8 px narrower, the boxes on their column). DialogToolOptions' Toolbar lists are
(R - 108) / 2 = 163 wide so Up, Down and Reset end on the page's padding, and `IntegralHeight = false` on its two list boxes (one line each
in InitializeComponent's body of DialogToolOptions.cs; a look property, no behaviour: the lists snapped to whole 15 px items stood 14 px
short of the padding). FormRecallList's View group ends 4 px after the check boxes' text extent as GridRulesTests reads it (16 + the GDI+
width + 2; the check box draws its text 10 px short of that, so 13 px after the text on both pages) and the Reactivations page's third
column boxes take the 8 px its shorter check box leaves: the window 1067 -> 1061 wide. FindTab's Search Direction ends 4 px after Down
(margins 9 and 12 px). ChartAxisCtl's check boxes on the bands' columns (118, the fx button column 188, the right band's 336). Rows rerun
on the planner's designers (the seven files rebuilt from the branch point with `<W>/plan-final.tsv`, checked to give the committed files

code with only AutoScaleBaseSize: the report designer's dialogs; RdlDesigner is per-monitor aware) is drawn at 150% scaled as a whole,
every font and Control.Scale(1.5), as Form.OnLoad's ApplyAutoScaling scales it at 144 dpi (about 1.47 against 100%), not with its fonts
only. PropertyDialog sets AutoScaleMode None (checked off screen with the .NET Framework's Windows Forms, whose old AutoScale code .NET keeps: a Form so built does not scale, nor a UserControl added to its tab page), so its
pages (ReportParameterCtl, StyleTextCtl, ChartAxisCtl and the others) are not scaled by the exe at 150%: their texts are cut there, on
main too. Their 150% pictures are left out of the gallery: an owner question. Also seen while checking: at 100% the same old AutoScale
grows the report designer's dialogs by about 1.3 (Segoe UI 9 against the 5 x 13 base of MS Sans Serif 8.25), so the exe shows them
roomier than the pictures, which draw the designer's size; no text is cut by it.

### Final consistency pass (UI-12F)

Branch `ui/ui12f-1` from `main` (`3d069cee0`, every slice merged). The lead's package UI-12F (`backlog-ui12-final-pass.md`): one rule
set for every slice. `<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12f-src/work`.

**1. The section gap over the bottom bar** (12 px, section 1). New rule `bar-gap` in `GridRulesTests.CheckBarGap`, for every grid window
of every slice: the bar is the row of the lowest buttons anchored to the bottom and not the top (or named OK, Save, Cancel, Close; the
anchors as the window's LayoutManager stores them, `GridDump.GetStoredAnchors`, since LayoutManager leaves every control at Top|Left),
the buttons and bottom-anchored controls that start on that row, the bottom-anchored rows of labels, fields and check boxes stacked 8 px
or less over it (FormConfirmationSetup's two status rows), a second row of bar buttons on the first row's columns
(FormRpTreatmentFinder) and a caption ending 0 to 4 px over a bar button (FormClaimPayTotal's "On all unpaid amounts:"). A row the bar
shares with other controls (a field row with Save beside it, a list or group beside a column of buttons down to the window's bottom:
FormDisplayFieldEdit, FormCounties, FormRpOutstandingIns) is no bottom bar. The content over the bar ends 12 px or more above it; more
is allowed (a window that keeps main's size shares its spare room out as gaps). The same reading in `grid_hand.bar_section_gap`, which
every layout script run now ends with (`KEEP_BAR_GAP = True` keeps a script's own) and which made the fix rows: on the tree of the build
before the fix (`<W>/tree-c2.tsv`), where the content lacks the gap, a grid, list or note gets shorter by what it lacks (it scrolls);
anything else keeps its height and the bar, what stands beside it and the window move down by as much (a window never shrinks). The
rows are hand rows of each window's slice file, rule `by hand (UI-12F): the bottom bar a section gap (12 px) under the content above
it`: **170 windows of every slice** (most were 8 px, the sibling gap, in the scripts of slices 2 to 11 and in four pilot tool windows).
Known: FormPerio (`grid-known/5.tsv`: its control named butSave is the Save to Images button of the right-hand column; no bar).
`OPMS_UI_GRID_BAR_GAPS` writes every window's gap as the test reads it (`<W>/bargaps-final.tsv`: 390 windows at 12 px, 233 above, 282
with no bar, FormPerio).

**2. A window never shrinks.** Test `GridRulesTests.GridWindows_NeverShrink`: every designer file a slice owns is at least main's size
before UI-12 in both directions, read from `Data/grid-main-sizes.tsv` (the 1008 "." rows of the layout snapshot of `79d92c89b`, the last
main before the pilot merged); `code_sized` names a direction the window's code sets at run time (FormTaskEdit's width, from the split
container in LoadTask; FormEmployeeSelect's height, the screen's). The ten that had shrunk are back:
- slice 10 (scripts, `_s10.keep_main`: main's size, the bar on the bottom padding, the room the content does not fill shared out as
  equal gaps between the blocks and over the bar, as slice 8 did): FormTrackNextSetup 376 wide (the group spans the content),
  FormRxAlertEdit 697 x 346, FormRepeatChargeEditMulti 645 x 305, FormTranslationEdit 856 x 621, FormReplicationEdit 353 high (its 796
  width stays; the seven rows one gap apart), FormSigElementDefEdit 599 x 363;
- slice 4 (scripts, and the fix round's hand rows): FormEhrEncryption 680 wide (both boxes wider and as tall as on main, 8 px margins),
  FormHL7DefMessageEdit 534 and FormPatientStatusTool 639 high (the bottom row on the padding, the grid 12 px over it, the column on the
  8 px padding on both sides);
- slice 1 (hand rows, no reproducible tree): FormPaySplitEdit 791 wide (the right column and the tab pages' right column 25 px over).

**3. The label-centre rule in every slice** (`IsLabelCentreChecked` gone): ControlChart's label8 to label12 and label15 (pilot script),

from the font (150% pictures). FormProgressViewer's MinimumSize and MaximumSize (which LayoutManager refuses in the designer) are set in
its OnLoad after FormODBase.OnLoad has applied the screen's dpi and sized the window (`SetSizeLimitsScaled`: 525 x 166 and 1000 x 800 at
96 dpi in LayoutManager's scale, always containing the window's size; in the constructor the scale is still 96 dpi's, the fix round's
finding). FormInstallAllocator_Provider's maroon REPORTS header and its maroon-on-white report buttons keep their colours
(`UiLooks.IsOwnColours`: a stock button whose designer gives both its back and fore colour for a text; only these four buttons in the
program's designers). Tests: `BuilderAndScanTests` (the five are LayoutManager windows, none is without; the builder's path without it
still tested), `FontLiteralScanTests.Scope_IsTheTierOneScansList` (their designer code exempt now). `grid-known/9.tsv`: the
GuarantorAllocationCheck `unframed` row gone (the frame is attached now), a FormProgressViewer `overlap` row added (main's docking of the
message label and the progress bar, seen through LayoutManager's placed controls).

ScaleToDpi (`BuiltWindow.IsPropertyDialogPage`, `PropertyDialogScalesPages`), so the before pictures show main's cut texts ("Nam",
"Alignm", "Directi") and the after pictures the scaled pages. The dialogs' old Form.AutoScale stays.

### Slice 15 (the report designer's and viewer's remaining dialogs and property pages)

Branch `ui/ui12-15-1` from `main` (`6f343389d`). The lead's list `slice15-windows.tsv` (the owner, 2026-11-24: "Fix all of them", for the
windows UI-11-FINAL's sweep listed as off the layout grid): 42 windows of the report designer (`RdlDesign`, a WinExe) and viewer
(`RdlViewer`), third-party-derived plain `Form`s and `UserControl`s whose designer code is in their `.cs` files (slice 13's reading and
editing of InitializeComponent's body; every licence header and every byte outside InitializeComponent's body stays, except the two code
changes below). `<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-15-src/work`.

**The windows** (`grid-windows/15.tsv`, slice 15, mode tool; their 42 `grid` rows out of `sweep-known.tsv`, so the sweep checks them):
- **Layout scripts** (26, `tools/UiFit/hand/<Window>.py` on `hand/_s15.py`, slice 15's common steps on slice 13's `_s13.py`: a label
  right-aligned before its field, a row of label + field + buttons, a stock check box or radio button with its 24 px lead, a caption over
  its box, a wrapping note, a property page's content edge at 440 x 292; each script's header says what the planner left and what it
  does; rows in `grid-hand/15.tsv`): ChartCtl, CustomReportItemCtl, DataSetRowsCtl, DataSetsCtl, DialogAbout, DialogDataSourceRef,
  DialogDataSources, DialogEmbeddedImages, DialogExprEditor, DialogFilterOperator, DialogListOfStrings, DialogNew, DialogNewMatrix,
  DialogValidValues, DialogValidateRdl, DrillParametersDialog, GroupingCtl, ImageCtl, MatrixCtl, PropertyDialog, SQLCtl,
  SingleCtlDialog, SubreportCtl, TableColumnCtl, and the viewer's DataSourcePassword and DialogMessages. The planner placed their rows
  but left the plain dialogs' bottom bars where they stood (OK and Cancel 15 to 30 px from the right edge, bands of 30 to 90 px under
  them), kept three captions run into their boxes (ChartCtl's percent width, DialogDataSourceRef's Data provider, DialogEmbeddedImages'
  name, DialogNewMatrix's cell expression) and widened four property pages past PropertyDialog's 450 px tab page (GroupingCtl 631,
  TableColumnCtl 555, MatrixCtl's boxes to 461, SubreportCtl's check box); main cut DataSetsCtl's Refresh Fields, DialogAbout's E-mail,
  DialogListOfStrings' and DialogValidateRdl's last note line, DataSetRowsCtl's Load From SQL (three lines) and spaced its buttons 9 to
  20 px apart.
- **The planner alone** (13, its rows only, each picture checked): BodyCtl, ChartLegendCtl, CodeCtl, FiltersCtl, ListCtl,
  ModulesClassesCtl, QueryParametersCtl, RdlDesigner, RdlEditPreview, ReportXmlCtl, SortingCtl, TableCtl, TableRowCtl. RdlDesigner, the
  MDI main window, was skipped by slice 13 because nothing of its designer is shown: its 21 designer buttons are hidden image holders the
  code copies into the toolbar it builds. It is on the grid now: the planner stacks the hidden holders in two columns inside the client
  area (the `hidden-frame` rule), and the window's menus, toolbar and status bar are built and docked by its code as before (a toolbar,
  not a form layout: unchanged).
- **No designer** (3, on the list so the rules and the sweep check them; nothing to place): RdlViewer's PageDrawing (draws the report's
  pages), RdlDesign's DesignXmlDraw (the design surface) and DesignEditLines (the XML editor's text). They hold no controls.

**What the code places** (each `.cs` read):
- DialogExprEditor's Layout handler placed the operator tree and the expression box from the window's outer size (`this.Width`,
  `this.Height`): the boxes ran into the right edge and to 4 px over OK and Cancel. It now lays them out from the client area with the
  grid's numbers (local constants named after `CodeBase.UiGrid`'s PAD, ITEM_GAP, CAPTION_GAP and SECTION_GAP: the report designer does not
  reference CodeBase): the two boxes share the width with ">>" 6 px between them at their top, each 2 px under its caption, down to a
  section gap over the bar; the behaviour (both fill the window as it is resized) is unchanged. The designer matches what the handler
  makes at main's 440 x 310.
- PropertyDialog docked its tab control over the window and DialogNew its choices list (Dock Fill), SQLCtl its tables tree (Dock Left):
  their frames lay on the window's edges (the owner's FormTasks example). Each now stands on the padding, anchored to the sides it was
  docked to (the same resizing); PropertyDialog's window is 16 px wider and 11 taller (474 x 377) so its tab control and its pages keep
  main's size (the pages slices 13 and 15 laid out for about 450 x 300). The bar panels of PropertyDialog, SingleCtlDialog, DialogNew and
  SQLCtl are 43 px: the buttons a section gap (12 px) under the content, on the bottom and right padding.
- PropertyDialog.AddTab sizes each tab page and docks the page in it (unchanged); `ScaleToDpi` (UI-12F) scales the dialog with its pages,
  the new bounds too. The grid widths of DataSetsCtl, FiltersCtl, GroupingCtl, ModulesClassesCtl, QueryParametersCtl, SortingCtl,
  SubreportCtl, DrillParametersDialog and DialogValidValues are columns of their grids (unchanged). RdlDesigner builds and docks its
  toolbar (unchanged). GroupingCtl hides its check boxes for some report items (the designer shows them all; laid out shown).

**Harness** (tests only, no product code): `SampleData.BeforePlainHost` gives DataSetRowsCtl (PropertyDialog's Data page) a data set
with no fields and an empty data table before the builder adds it to its test form (its VisibleChanged compares the two; UI-11-FINAL
listed it as one of the four types the harness could not build). FinalSweepTests' list of unbuildable types loses DataSetRowsCtl (the
sweep builds and checks it). `FitGallery.GetSliceFifteenTabState`: RdlEditPreview's RDL Text and Preview pages.
`SliceDataTests.Batches_EachFileOnceNoneLaidOut`: the floor of more than 100 batched files (UI-12T) is gone, since slices 14 to 16 lay out
the last windows; every batched file must exist instead. `UI-12-batches.tsv` rerun (`plan_batches.py`: the 39 designer files out).

**Window sizes** (client area; a property page's size is its UserControl's): main's size kept by 37; grown: PropertyDialog 458 x 366 ->
474 x 377 (the tab control on the padding, its pages as on main), DialogFilterOperator 256 x 198 -> 274 x 201 (its label on one line),
DialogValidateRdl 503 x 261 -> 503 x 275 (the note's third line), CodeCtl 472 x 288 -> 472 x 299 and TableCtl 472 x 288 -> 472 x 291
(the planner's section gaps). No window shrinks (`GridWindows_NeverShrink`).

**Rows.** `grid-hand/15.tsv`: the 26 scripts' rows. `grid-known/15.tsv`: none (no finding of the rules stays). `grid-runtime-texts/15.tsv`:
none (no label or check box text set in code in these windows). `fit-fixes-superseded/15.tsv`: none. `layout-snapshot/15.tsv` written
from the final build. Re-plan from the final build's tree (`<W>/tree-a3-100.tsv`, `<W>/replan-a3.tsv`): 157 rows, all in 22 of the 26

**Commands** (worktree root; `<lane>` = the lane's odbuild.ps1):
```
rem grid-windows/15.tsv from the lead's list; the whole filter, then RdlViewer, ReportDesigner and UiRefreshTests through the lane (x64)
OPMS_UI_GRID_DUMP=<W>/tree-before-100.tsv OPMS_UI_GRID_WINDOWS=<the 42> OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY=<W>/before-100 OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY_FONT=program (Targeted UiRefreshTests)
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py plan --tree <W>/tree-before-100.tsv --windows <the 42> --out <W>/plan.tsv --unplaced <W>/unplaced.tsv --tree-out <W>/tree-planout.tsv
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py apply --plan <W>/plan.tsv
python tools/UiFit/grid_hand.py --tree <W>/tree-planout.tsv --window <each script window>   (DataSetRowsCtl: --tree <W>/tree-a1-100.tsv, main's layout: it first built there)
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py apply --plan tests/UiRefreshTests/Data/grid-hand/15.tsv
rem the Dock lines of PropertyDialog, DialogNew and SQLCtl and DialogExprEditor's Layout handler by hand; build
OPMS_UI_GRID_DUMP=<W>/tree-a3-100.tsv OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY=<W>/a3-100 OPMS_UI_LAYOUT_SNAPSHOT_WRITE=1; <W>/a3-150 at 1.5 for the densest
python tools/UiFit/plan_batches.py
```

**Fix round** (branch `ui/ui12-15-2` from `ui/ui12-15-1`; the review's 14 points, all real, each fixed; `<W>/f3-100`, `<W>/f3-150`):
- **The tab page's width** (points 1, 2, 6, 7). PropertyDialog docks each page in a tab page of about 450 px, so a page's content ends
  at 440 (`_s15.PAGE_R`); the planner had widened CodeCtl's panel (the error list and code box) and TableRowCtl's Visibility group to
  456 (8 to 464). In the tab page the code box's vertical scroll bar showed 3 px and the group's right border was cut; the audit drew the
  pages at their own 472 px and missed it. Both have layout scripts now (`hand/CodeCtl.py`, `hand/TableRowCtl.py`): CodeCtl's panel 8
  to 440 with Check Syntax on its right edge (358), TableRowCtl's group 8 to 440 as TableColumnCtl's (fx 8 px inside it, the boxes
  after one right-aligned label column) and Row Height laid out as Column Width (its label right-aligned 6 px before its box on the
  group's field column, 182). The audit now checks every page against the 440 px edge.
- **GroupingCtl** (points 3, 4, 5). Name, the Group By grid, Label and Parent end on one right edge (386) and their buttons start on one
  column 6 px after it (392); the grid's fx is 23 px tall as the other fx buttons, Delete, Up and Down a field tall (23), stacked 4 px
  apart under it with the grid as tall as the stack (104); Label a section gap under them (the equal-gaps rule: an 8 px gap after the
  4 px stack was a finding); the second column of check boxes on one x (212: the widest first-column box and the item gap).
- **List boxes** (points 9, 10, 11). DialogValidateRdl's error list, DialogEmbeddedImages' image list and DialogNewMatrix's three lists
  snapped the heights the scripts gave them down to whole items (IntegralHeight), so they ended 12 to 14 px short of the bar, of Remove
  and of each other. They have IntegralHeight false (a hand row: `Hand.integral_height_off` in `grid_hand.py`; the applier writes the
  `IntegralHeight` line, tested in `test_uifit_tools.CodeFileTests`): the error list 12 px over the bar, the image list ends with Remove,
  the fields list and Matrix Rows on one bottom (307).
- **Captions** (points 12, 13). DialogAbout's version lines are as wide as the title (240, 184) and centre under it. Data provider
  (DialogDataSources, DialogDataSourceRef) and DataSet (DialogNewMatrix) are single labels with no label column to align to: at the
  content edge now (MiddleLeft, as wide as their text, `_s15.lead`), their boxes 6 px after the text.
- **150%** (point 14). The harness's 150% font pass for the program's windows (`GridStyleGallery.ScaleFontsAsWindowsDoes`) scales the
  labels, buttons, check boxes and group boxes only, since LayoutManager gives a program window's inputs their fonts; on a property page
  it left the text boxes and combo boxes at the top level at their 100% text and height beside 150% labels, while those in a group box
  took the group's font. A property page is per-monitor aware and Windows draws every point size at the dpi, so the gallery now scales
  every font of a property page (`FitGallery.ScaleEveryFont`, tests only; no product change). Label and box text on one line within
  1 px (TableCtl at 150%: No rows message 63 / 62, Detail Element Name 348 / 348). The before pictures at 150% keep the earlier pass.

  was), applied; `layout-snapshot/15.tsv` rewritten. Gallery `G-UI3-ui12-15` rebuilt: 9 pictures at 100% and the 7 at 150% redrawn, the

### Slice 16 (the drawn surfaces and the shared controls)

Branch `ui/ui12-16-1` from `main` (`6f343389d`); review fix round `ui/ui12-16-2` (the end of this section). The lead's list `slice16-windows.tsv` (54 windows; the owner, 2026-11-24: "Fix all of
them" for the windows UI-11-FINAL's sweep listed as off the grid, `sweep-known.tsv` rule `grid`): the windows built around a surface the
code draws (the appointment and imaging modules, print previews, browsers, the claim form and sheet editors, the tooth chart windows,
signatures) and the shared controls. Slices 14 and 15 ran at the same time; none of their windows is here. Every one of the 54 is in
`grid-windows/16.tsv` and its `grid` row is out of `sweep-known.tsv` (FinalSweepTests now checks it). `<W>` =
`.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-16-src/work`.

**The rule of the slice.** The surface stays exactly as its code draws it; around it the grid: the surface 8 px (`UiGrid.PAD`) from the
window's edges where the window allows (under a docked toolbar: 8 px under it), a section gap (12 px) over the bottom bar, 8 px beside a
column of buttons. Docked toolbars stay edge to edge (window chrome). Where the code places the surface or the controls around it, the code
now uses UiGrid's numbers with the same behaviour; the designer is laid out to match (one layout script per window, `hand/_s16.py` for the
common steps), so the harness draws what the code shows.

**Code changed** (each with a `UI-12 slice 16` comment):
- Print previews: `FormRpPrintPreview` (Load), `FormRpStatement` (Layout: the page buttons' panel centred 8 px over the bottom),
  `FormClaimPrint` (Layout: 8 px before the page buttons' column), `FormPrntScrn` (Layout), `FormReportComplex` (`LayoutPreview`, used by
  RefreshWindow and SizeChanged: the 28 px empty band under the preview is gone), `FormTPsign` (`LayoutPreview`, between the toolbar and
  the signature panel; the Change User button and the signer's name end 6 px before the box, the button under the caption, the name 2 px
  under it).
- `FormTerminal` (the kiosk): its column keeps the code's centring, with the grid's gaps (a section gap under the welcome, the caption gap
  over the list, a sibling gap under it, the item gap over Done; the controls touched or stood 23 px apart).
- `ControlAppt.LayoutControls`: the book and the calendar column 8 px from the module's edges and 8 px apart, the tabs 8 px under the
  column, the calendar 4 px under the date, the lower panel 4 px under the calendar and 351 px tall (317: its rows at the 27 px pitch);
  ShowSearch puts the search group 4 px under the pinboard.
- `ControlImages.LayoutControls`: the sliders from 8 px with 6 px between them and the paint toolbar; the tree, the Pearl panel (70 px,
  from 62: its group on the grid), the image area, the unmounted bar and the note panel 8 px from the module's edges and 8 px apart (they
  stood edge to edge); the drawing tools' toolbar stays on the image area's top edge.
- `ControlVideoPlayer` (the bar: 8 px padding, 6 px between the scrub bar, the buttons and the message), `ControlCameraSession` (UiGrid
  constants for its 8 px; the progress line 2 px under the title), `FormImageViewer` (UiGrid.PAD for its info line's padding).
- `ODDatePicker.OnSizeChanged`: the date box at y 0 (it was at 1 in a 23 px picker, its 23 px box cut by 1 px); the 63 px label area every
  host aligns on stays.
- `FormToothChartingBig`: unchanged, as on main (the chart Dock Fill, edge to edge). The tooth chart's look and behaviour match Open
  Dental's newest release (owner rule, no fork-only rules), where this window is the chart edge to edge; an 8 px frame would draw every

### Slice 14 (the windows laid out by their code)

Branch `ui/ui12-14-1` from `main` (`6f343389d`). The owner's "Fix all of them" for the 124 windows UI-11-FINAL's sweep left off the grid
(`sweep-known.tsv` rule `grid`); the lead's list `slice14-windows.tsv` has 28 of them: the main window, the task list, the Family module, the
editors and report viewers whose code lays them out, the windows with states, the message boxes, the progress windows, the separate
programs' windows (DbSettings, ServiceManager, UpdateFileCopier) and the imaging windows built in code. Each reason of the list was a
problem to solve: all 28 are on the grid (`grid-windows/14.tsv`, mode tool) and their `grid` rows left `sweep-known.tsv`; none kept.
Slices 15 and 16 ran at the same time with their own lists. `<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-ui12-14-src/work`.

**Code that places controls, on the grid's numbers** (`CodeBase.UiGrid`; the behaviour kept):
- `UserControlTasks` (FormTasks, the main window's task list): Resize and LayoutTreeAndGrid put the tab strip, the calendar, the tree and the
  grid at x 0 over the full width, edge to edge under the tabs (the owner's picture: the boxes on the window's edges). Now `UiGrid.PAD`
  from the sides, the tab strip a row gap under the filter row (`TOP_TABS`), the calendar a row gap under the strip (`TOP_CALENDAR`), the
  tree and the grid `UiGrid.ROW_GAP` apart, the grid to the bottom padding (also FillGrid's height). The calendar shows on the date tabs
  only (`monthCalendar.Visible`): the tree and grid covered it on the other tabs, and the gaps would show it. The calendar's control
  type is untouched (package UI-CAL); its bounds only.
- `ControlFamily`: the insurance grid's place beside the super family and clones pane is one method, `LayoutSuperClones` (2 px -> the
  modules' 4 px, `UiGrid.ROW_GAP`), called where RefreshModuleScreen placed it twice.
- `FormWikiEdit` (ResizeControls): the markup box and the preview `UiGrid.PAD` under the two toolbars and from the edges, a sibling gap
  apart (main: edge to edge, 52 px from the top, 2 px apart).
- `FormQuery` (Load): the report's preview on the padding over the bar's 24 px and a section gap (main: at 0, 0, 39 px short of the
  bottom). The split the user drags: `Panel1MinSize` 126 (the button column: Favorites, Add To Favorites, Copy / Paste, Submit Query,
  Run on report server) and `Panel2MinSize` 150 (the Title row, the caption and four grid rows), two designer lines, so both panes stay
  whole at any split; the query box and the results grid follow the split by their anchors (gallery state `split`).
- `FormOpenDental` (LayoutControls): the signal grid a section gap under the module bar's buttons (`ModuleBar.GetButtonsHeight`, new:
  the buttons' 57 + 1 px each); main's fixed 489 px stood under the taller 65 px buttons of Open Dental's bar (59 px of empty bar over the
  grid). The modules, the module bar, the toolbar and the docked tasks stay edge to edge (a main window's parts meet); the 489 stays while
  the bar has no buttons.
- `FormUpdate` (Load): the window ends a padding under the message (the fork shows nothing under it; the version groups and the classic
  panel are Open Dental's states, kept and laid out in the designer).
- `ServiceManager.FormWebConfigSettings` (constructor): a service other than replication shortens the window by the hidden rows
  (`groupReplicationMaster.Bottom - textPEM.Bottom`), not to a fixed 375 px.
- `ODProgressBar` (LayoutHelper): its 6 px padding is `UiGrid.ITEM_GAP`.
- Built in code on the grid's constants: `FormCameraSettings` (fields and labels 23 px, the check boxes 18 px, each section a section
  gap under the one before, the bar a section gap under the last row), `FormImageEnhance` (the slider rows one pitch apart, the check
  boxes a list, the two rows of buttons one bottom bar: anchored to the bottom, All in mount over Close, a section gap under the note),
  `FormToothPicker` (the bar a section gap under the last row, the slot check box a check box row), `OpenPmsDentalDbSettings`'
  `FormDbSettings` (one label column, label + field rows from the padding, the credential group's choices with what each sets under its
  text, the status down to a section gap over the bar) and `FormSecretPrompt` (the prompt a caption gap over its box, the bar a section
  gap under it).

**Commands** (worktree root; `<lane>` = the lane's odbuild.ps1; the run's variables as Run-Slice.ps1 sets them):
```
rem the whole filter through the lane (x64); OPMS_UI_GRID_DUMP=<W>/tree-before.tsv, OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY=<W>/before-100 (the 28), before-100s (the states), before-150 (1.5)
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py plan --tree <W>/tree-before.tsv --windows <the 28> --out <W>/plan2.tsv --unplaced <W>/unplaced1.tsv
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py apply --plan <W>/plan-final.tsv   (FormServiceManage, FormWebConfigSettings)
python tools/UiFit/grid_hand.py --tree <W>/tree-before.tsv --window <each of the 16 scripts>
python tools/UiFit/grid_tools.py apply --plan tests/UiRefreshTests/Data/grid-hand/14.tsv   (designers at the branch point)
rem build; OPMS_UI_GRID_DUMP=<W>/tree-a4.tsv OPMS_UI_FIT_GALLERY=<W>/a4-100 OPMS_UI_LAYOUT_SNAPSHOT_WRITE=1; a4-150 at 1.5 for the densest
python tools/UiFit/plan_batches.py
```

### UI-CLOSE (the last layout items)

Branch `ui/uiclose-1` from `main` (`394e5ecd5`), the lead's package UI-CLOSE: the five designer files left in
[UI-12-batches.tsv](UI-12-batches.tsv), FormServiceManage's empty band and FormBackup's cut example notes. Package UI-BRAND-2 ran at the
same time and changes FormBackup's `textBox5` text (`OpenPmsImages`): this package changes only layout lines of `FormBackup.designer.cs`
(`git merge-tree` of the two branches: clean). `<W>` = `.git/od-tools/test-results/G-UI3-uiclose-src/work`.

**1. The batch list ends empty.** The five files had no designer controls (`controls` 0):
- `RdlDesign/MDIChild.cs` (fyiReporting.RdlDesign.MDIChild, the report designer's document window): the report editor (RdlEditPreview,
  slice 15) docked to fill it, made in its constructor; nothing to lay out. On the grid list as **slice 17** (`grid-windows/17.tsv`, mode
  tool; GridRulesTests builds and checks it: no finding), its snapshot row in `layout-snapshot/17.tsv` (292 x 266, as on main; the row in
  `unsliced.tsv` stays for the lead's `split_grid_data.py` rerun, which prunes it). The sweep still leaves it out (only ever an MDI child).
- Four files the harness cannot build (no Form or UserControl, so no window key of `WindowBuilder.GetScanTypes`, and GridRulesTests would
  fail them as "not a scanned type"): each is a row of `sweep-known.tsv` with rule `grid`, key `(window)` and its reason:
  `OpenPmsDental.FormODBase` (the base of every program window: no InitializeComponent by design, its comment says the designer values
  would become every derived window's defaults; no controls; confirmed: it has no layout of its own, it draws the frame and title bar
  and every derived window is laid out), `OpenPmsDental.UI.UnmountedBar` (the Windows Forms bar of unmounted pictures; the Images module
  uses `WpfControls.UI.UnmountedBar`; it draws its header and pictures, its four buttons are the header's strip its code places; kept, not
  cut), `OpenPmsDental.UI.WindowingSlider` (draws itself; its host FormImagingSetup places it) and `OpenPmsDental.DashFamilyInsurance`
  (the patient dashboard's Family Insurance cell: draws its text and boxes at the size the dashboard's sheet gives it, like DashApptGrid
  and UserControlScreenTooth).
- `tools/UiFit/plan_batches.py`: `nothing_to_lay_out` reads those `grid` rows and leaves their designer files out of the batches (a
  window the harness builds goes on the grid list instead). Rerun: **0 designer files in 0 batches**; `UI-12-batches.tsv` is its header.
